   191	@functools.partial(jax.custom_vjp, nondiff_argnums=(2,))
   192	def _gather_dispatch_rows(
   193	    x_local: Float[Array, "Tlocal H"], sorted_indices: Int[Array, "TK"], topk: int
   194	) -> Float[Array, "TK H"]:
   195	    """Build the expert-sorted dispatch buffer with one gather.
   196	
   197	    Equivalent to ``jnp.repeat(x_local, topk, axis=0)[sorted_indices]`` without
   198	    materializing the repeated buffer or running a data-sized permute. The backward pass
   199	    is the transpose: each token sums the cotangent rows of its ``topk`` sorted slots.
   200	    """
   201	    return x_local[sorted_indices // topk]
   202	
   203	
   204	def _gather_dispatch_rows_fwd(
   205	    x_local: Float[Array, "Tlocal H"], sorted_indices: Int[Array, "TK"], topk: int
   206	) -> tuple[Float[Array, "TK H"], Int[Array, "TK"]]:
   207	    return _gather_dispatch_rows(x_local, sorted_indices, topk), sorted_indices
   208	
   209	
   210	def _gather_dispatch_rows_bwd(
   211	    topk: int, sorted_indices: Int[Array, "TK"], cotangent: Float[Array, "TK H"]
   212	) -> tuple[Float[Array, "Tlocal H"], None]:
   213	    tokens_per_shard = sorted_indices.shape[0] // topk
   214	    positions = jnp.argsort(sorted_indices).reshape(tokens_per_shard, topk)
   215	    if sonic_gather_sum_available():
   216	        ones = jnp.ones((tokens_per_shard, topk), dtype=jnp.float32)
   217	        grad_x = sonic_gather_sum(cotangent, positions, ones)
   218	    else:
   219	        grad_x = jnp.sum(cotangent[positions], axis=1, dtype=jnp.float32)
   220	    return grad_x.astype(cotangent.dtype), None
   221	
   222	
   223	_gather_dispatch_rows.defvjp(_gather_dispatch_rows_fwd, _gather_dispatch_rows_bwd)
   224	
   225	
   226	def _loop_local_zeros(
   227	    rows: int, hidden_dim: int, dtype, tie: Int[Array, "..."], site: int = 0
   228	) -> Float[Array, "rows H"]:
   229	    """Zero buffer for an in-place ``ragged_all_to_all`` output slot, built so XLA never copies it.
   230	
   231	    ``ragged_all_to_all`` writes its result in place into the output-init operand. A literal
   232	    ``jnp.zeros`` init is a trace-time constant: it gets hoisted out of the layer scan (and
   233	    identical inits CSE together), so CopyInsertion must mint a fresh multi-GB device copy of
   234	    the pristine zeros into the a2a's output slot on every scan iteration. Deriving the zeros
   235	    from ``tie`` -- a per-site, per-iteration traced value -- keeps each init inside the loop
   236	    body and distinct from every other init, so it lowers to one write-only fill kernel whose
   237	    buffer the a2a updates directly.
   238	
   239	    ``tie`` must be a non-negative integer array (group or transfer sizes): with ``site >= 0``,
   240	    ``min(tie[0], -site) + site`` is exactly zero at runtime but not foldable at compile time.
   241	
   242	    ``site`` must be distinct per call site. Two sites can hold the SAME tie tensor without it
   243	    being obvious (dispatch send_sizes and return recv_sizes are one tensor in ep_common), and
   244	    same-shape same-tie fills CSE into one value with two destructive in-place a2a consumers --
   245	    which makes CopyInsertion mint back the multi-GB copy this helper exists to prevent.
   246	    """
   247	    zero = (jnp.minimum(tie.reshape(-1)[0], -site) + site).astype(dtype)
   248	    return jax.lax.broadcast(zero, (rows, hidden_dim))
   249	
   250	
   251	@functools.partial(jax.custom_vjp, nondiff_argnums=(0, 1))
   252	def _dispatch_ragged_a2a(
   253	    capacity: int,
   254	    source_rows: int,
   255	    chunk_source: Float[Array, "TK H"],
   256	    input_offsets: Int[Array, "U"],
   257	    send_sizes: Int[Array, "U"],
   258	    output_offsets: Int[Array, "U"],
   259	    recv_sizes: Int[Array, "U"],
   260	) -> Float[Array, "C H"]:
   261	    """Dispatch-direction ``ragged_all_to_all`` into a freshly zeroed receiver buffer.
   262	
   263	    Owning the output-init here (instead of taking a caller-built ``jnp.zeros``) lets both the
   264	    primal and the transposed direction build their inits with `_loop_local_zeros`. jax's
   265	    built-in transpose rule inits the swapped a2a with plain zeros, which XLA hoists out of
   266	    the layer loop and re-copies every iteration; the custom vjp below is that same rule with
   267	    the init construction replaced. Zeros stay load-bearing: receiver rows no peer writes must
   268	    read 0 downstream, and dropped source rows must read a 0 cotangent in the backward.
   269	    """
   270	    del source_rows
   271	    output_init = _loop_local_zeros(capacity, chunk_source.shape[1], chunk_source.dtype, send_sizes, site=1)
   272	    return jax.lax.ragged_all_to_all(
   273	        chunk_source, output_init, input_offsets, send_sizes, output_offsets, recv_sizes, axis_name="expert"
   274	    )
   275	
   276	
   277	def _dispatch_ragged_a2a_fwd(
   278	    capacity: int,
   279	    source_rows: int,
   280	    chunk_source: Float[Array, "TK H"],
   281	    input_offsets: Int[Array, "U"],
   282	    send_sizes: Int[Array, "U"],
   283	    output_offsets: Int[Array, "U"],
   284	    recv_sizes: Int[Array, "U"],
   285	) -> tuple[Float[Array, "C H"], tuple[jax.Array, jax.Array, jax.Array, jax.Array]]:
   286	    result = _dispatch_ragged_a2a(
   287	        capacity, source_rows, chunk_source, input_offsets, send_sizes, output_offsets, recv_sizes
   288	    )
   289	    return result, (input_offsets, send_sizes, output_offsets, recv_sizes)
   290	
   291	
   292	def _dispatch_ragged_a2a_bwd(
   293	    capacity: int,
   294	    source_rows: int,
   295	    residuals: tuple[jax.Array, jax.Array, jax.Array, jax.Array],
   296	    cotangent: Float[Array, "C H"],
   297	) -> tuple[Float[Array, "TK H"], None, None, None, None]:
   298	    del capacity
   299	    input_offsets, send_sizes, output_offsets, recv_sizes = residuals
   300	    # The transpose of a ragged all-to-all is the reverse ragged all-to-all: swap the send and
   301	    # recv roles and exchange the offset vectors across shards, exactly as jax's transpose rule
   302	    # does. Source rows that were clipped receive no cotangent and keep the init's zeros.
   303	    output_offsets_x = jax.lax.all_to_all(output_offsets, "expert", 0, 0, tiled=True)
   304	    input_offsets_x = jax.lax.all_to_all(input_offsets, "expert", 0, 0, tiled=True)
   305	    # Tie to this side's send_sizes, not recv_sizes: the return path's passthrough fill ties to
   306	    # its own send_sizes, which is the SAME tensor as this dispatch's recv_sizes, and two fills
   307	    # with identical shape and tie CSE back into one value with a destructive consumer --
   308	    # re-minting exactly the copy this construction removes.
   309	    init = _loop_local_zeros(source_rows, cotangent.shape[1], cotangent.dtype, send_sizes, site=2)
   310	    source_ct = jax.lax.ragged_all_to_all(
   311	        cotangent, init, output_offsets_x, recv_sizes, input_offsets_x, send_sizes, axis_name="expert"
   312	    )
   313	    return source_ct, None, None, None, None
   314	
   315	
   316	_dispatch_ragged_a2a.defvjp(_dispatch_ragged_a2a_fwd, _dispatch_ragged_a2a_bwd)
   317	
   318	
   319	@functools.partial(jax.custom_vjp, nondiff_argnums=(0,))
   320	def _return_ragged_a2a(
   321	    capacity: int,
   322	    out_dispatch: Float[Array, "C H"],
   323	    returned: Float[Array, "TK H"],
   324	    input_offsets: Int[Array, "U"],
   325	    send_sizes: Int[Array, "U"],
   326	    output_offsets: Int[Array, "U"],
   327	    recv_sizes: Int[Array, "U"],
   328	) -> Float[Array, "TK H"]:
   329	    """Return-direction ``ragged_all_to_all``: write expert outputs back over ``returned``.
   330	
   331	    Same construction as `_dispatch_ragged_a2a`: the primal is unchanged, and the custom vjp
   332	    replicates jax's transpose rule operation for operation, replacing only the transposed
   333	    a2a's zero init so it is built per-iteration instead of hoisted and copied.
   334	    """
   335	    del capacity
   336	    return jax.lax.ragged_all_to_all(
   337	        out_dispatch, returned, input_offsets, send_sizes, output_offsets, recv_sizes, axis_name="expert"
   338	    )
   339	
   340	
   341	def _return_ragged_a2a_fwd(
   342	    capacity: int,
   343	    out_dispatch: Float[Array, "C H"],
   344	    returned: Float[Array, "TK H"],
   345	    input_offsets: Int[Array, "U"],
   346	    send_sizes: Int[Array, "U"],
   347	    output_offsets: Int[Array, "U"],
   348	    recv_sizes: Int[Array, "U"],
   349	) -> tuple[Float[Array, "TK H"], tuple[jax.Array, jax.Array, jax.Array, jax.Array]]:
   350	    result = _return_ragged_a2a(
   351	        capacity, out_dispatch, returned, input_offsets, send_sizes, output_offsets, recv_sizes
   352	    )
   353	    return result, (input_offsets, send_sizes, output_offsets, recv_sizes)
   354	
   355	
   356	def _return_ragged_a2a_bwd(
   357	    capacity: int,
   358	    residuals: tuple[jax.Array, jax.Array, jax.Array, jax.Array],
   359	    cotangent: Float[Array, "TK H"],
   360	) -> tuple[Float[Array, "C H"], Float[Array, "TK H"], None, None, None, None]:
   361	    input_offsets, send_sizes, output_offsets, recv_sizes = residuals
   362	    output_offsets_x = jax.lax.all_to_all(output_offsets, "expert", 0, 0, tiled=True)
   363	    input_offsets_x = jax.lax.all_to_all(input_offsets, "expert", 0, 0, tiled=True)
   364	    init = _loop_local_zeros(capacity, cotangent.shape[1], cotangent.dtype, recv_sizes, site=3)
   365	    out_dispatch_ct = jax.lax.ragged_all_to_all(
   366	        cotangent, init, output_offsets_x, recv_sizes, input_offsets_x, send_sizes, axis_name="expert"
   367	    )
   368	    # Rows this a2a overwrote took their value from ``out_dispatch``, so the pass-through
   369	    # cotangent to ``returned`` is zeroed on exactly the written intervals. This mirrors jax's
   370	    # transpose rule op for op (interval marks, cumsum, integer select_n) so the behavior is
   371	    # bit-identical, including on degenerate empty-group offset patterns.
   372	    interval_marks = (
   373	        jnp.zeros(cotangent.shape[0], jnp.int32).at[output_offsets_x].set(1).at[output_offsets_x + recv_sizes].add(-1)
   374	    )
   375	    written = jnp.broadcast_to(jnp.cumsum(interval_marks)[:, None], cotangent.shape)
   376	    passthrough_zero = _loop_local_zeros(cotangent.shape[0], cotangent.shape[1], cotangent.dtype, send_sizes, site=4)
   377	    returned_ct = jax.lax.select_n(written, cotangent, passthrough_zero)
   378	    return out_dispatch_ct, returned_ct, None, None, None, None
   379	
   380	
   381	_return_ragged_a2a.defvjp(_return_ragged_a2a_fwd, _return_ragged_a2a_bwd)
   382	
   383	
   384	def _resolve_expert_chunks(expert_chunks: int | None, local_experts: int) -> int:
   385	    """Sequential chunk count for a bank of ``local_experts``.
   386	
   387	    ``None`` takes `RAGGED_DEFAULT_EXPERT_CHUNKS` and falls back to one chunk on a bank the
   388	    default does not divide, which is how a mesh holding a single local expert runs. An explicit
   389	    count is a measurement knob, so an indivisible one raises instead of quietly running -- and
   390	    reporting -- a different chunk count than the one that was asked for.
   391	    """
   392	    if expert_chunks is None:
   393	        return RAGGED_DEFAULT_EXPERT_CHUNKS if local_experts % RAGGED_DEFAULT_EXPERT_CHUNKS == 0 else 1
   394	    if expert_chunks < 1:
   395	        raise ValueError(f"expert_chunks must be positive, got {expert_chunks}")
   396	    if local_experts % expert_chunks != 0:
   397	        raise ValueError(f"expert_chunks={expert_chunks} must divide the local expert count={local_experts}")
   398	    return expert_chunks
   399	
   400	
   401	def _moe_mlp_ep_ragged_a2a_local(
   402	    x_local: Float[Array, "Tlocal H"],
   403	    selected_experts_local: Int[Array, "Tlocal K"],
   404	    combine_weights_local: Float[Array, "Tlocal K"],
   405	    moe_w13_local: Float[Array, "Elocal H I2"],
   406	    moe_w2_local: Float[Array, "Elocal I H"],
   407	    *,
   408	    activation_fn: Callable[[jax.Array], jax.Array],
   409	    num_experts: int,
   410	    capacity_factor: float,
   411	    expert_chunks: int | None = None,
   412	) -> tuple[Float[Array, "Tlocal H"], CapacityOverflow]:
   413	    local_experts = moe_w13_local.shape[0]
   414	    if num_experts % local_experts != 0:
   415	        raise ValueError(
   416	            f"num_experts={num_experts} must be divisible by local expert count={local_experts} in EP mode"
   417	        )
   418	
   419	    shard_id = jax.lax.axis_index("expert")
   420	    ep_size = num_experts // local_experts
   421	    tokens_per_shard = x_local.shape[0]
   422	    topk = selected_experts_local.shape[1]
   423	    assignments_per_shard = tokens_per_shard * topk
   424	    local_capacity = int(math.ceil(capacity_factor * assignments_per_shard))
   425	    local_capacity = max(local_experts, local_capacity)
   426	
   427	    # Local experts are processed in sequential chunks so only one chunk's buffers are live at a
   428	    # time: capacity splits evenly across chunks, so two chunks halve the [C, H] transport
   429	    # buffers and the expert MLP's [C, I2] gate-up intermediate that sit between them. It also
   430	    # makes drop clipping per-chunk, which is a routing difference, not just a memory one.
   431	    #
   432	    # What chunking does not change is what crosses the forward/backward boundary. The block's
   433	    # remat policy re-runs this function in the backward, so the lowered module carries every
   434	    # ragged all-to-all three times at any chunk count -- primal, backward recompute, transpose,
   435	    # verified in the lowered StableHLO: 12 ops at two chunks, 6 at one. The a2a outputs are
   436	    # transient inside a chunk, not residuals pinned across the block window.
   437	    chunks = _resolve_expert_chunks(expert_chunks, local_experts)
   438	    chunk_experts = local_experts // chunks
   439	    chunk_capacity = max(chunk_experts, int(math.ceil(local_capacity / chunks)))
   440	    hidden_dim = x_local.shape[1]
   441	
   442	    with jax.named_scope("dispatch"):
   443	        flat_selected = selected_experts_local.reshape(-1)  # [TK]
   444	        sorted_indices = tree_checkpoint_name(jnp.argsort(flat_selected), RAGGED_SORTED_INDICES_REMAT_NAME)  # [TK]
   445	        group_sizes = tree_checkpoint_name(
   446	            jnp.bincount(flat_selected, length=num_experts).astype(jnp.int32), RAGGED_GROUP_SIZES_REMAT_NAME
   447	        )  # [E]
   448	        sorted_x = _gather_dispatch_rows(x_local, sorted_indices, topk)  # [TK, H]
   449	        all_group_sizes = jax.lax.all_gather(group_sizes, "expert")  # [S, E]
   450	
   451	    expert_mlp = _select_expert_mlp(activation_fn)
   452	    chunk_of_expert = (jnp.arange(num_experts, dtype=jnp.int32) % local_experts) // chunk_experts  # [E]
   453	    # Built per-iteration (not jnp.zeros) so the layer scan does not re-copy a hoisted
   454	    # constant into the in-place return a2a's output slot every step; see _loop_local_zeros.
   455	    # The zeros are load-bearing: rows no peer writes back must read 0 in the combine.
   456	    returned = _loop_local_zeros(assignments_per_shard, hidden_dim, x_local.dtype, group_sizes, site=5)  # [TK, H]
   457	    accepted_local = jnp.zeros((), dtype=jnp.int32)
   458	    for chunk_index in range(chunks):
   459	        with jax.named_scope(f"moe_chunk_{chunk_index}"):
   460	            chunk_all_group_sizes = jnp.where(chunk_of_expert[None, :] == chunk_index, all_group_sizes, 0)  # [S, E]
   461	            clipped_group_sizes = _clip_receiver_group_sizes(
   462	                chunk_all_group_sizes,
   463	                local_expert_size=local_experts,
   464	                receiver_capacity=chunk_capacity,
   465	            )
   466	            # Sender starts come from the full (unmasked) sizes, so each chunk reads its
   467	            # groups' accepted prefixes in place in the shared sorted buffer.
   468	            dispatch_params, return_params = _expert_granular_a2a_params(
   469	                all_group_sizes,
   470	                clipped_group_sizes,
   471	                shard_id,
   472	                local_expert_size=local_experts,
   473	            )
   474	            # Serialize the chunks. Without this barrier, the scheduler can start the dispatch
   475	            # of every chunk at the same time. This causes the high memory use that the chunks
   476	            # prevent. A variant that overlaps one transport with the MLP stays within memory.
   477	            # But it does not increase the speed. The transport and the MLP compete for the
   478	            # same SMs.
   479	            chunk_source, _ = jax.lax.optimization_barrier((sorted_x, returned))
   480	            # Accepted rows are the prefix of each unclipped expert group and receiver offsets
   481	            # pack arrivals expert-major, so the received buffer feeds the grouped MLP
   482	            # directly: no sender compaction and no receiver-side permute.
   483	            x_dispatch = _dispatch_ragged_a2a(  # [C, H]
   484	                chunk_capacity,
   485	                assignments_per_shard,
   486	                chunk_source,
   487	                *dispatch_params,
   488	            )
   489	            active_all = jnp.sum(  # [Elocal]
   490	                clipped_group_sizes.reshape(ep_size, ep_size, local_experts)[:, shard_id, :], axis=0
   491	            )
   492	            active_group_sizes = active_all[
   493	                chunk_index * chunk_experts : (chunk_index + 1) * chunk_experts
   494	            ]  # [Echunk]
   495	            total_valid = jnp.sum(active_group_sizes, dtype=jnp.int32)
   496	            physical_group_sizes = active_group_sizes.at[-1].add(chunk_capacity - total_valid)  # [Echunk]
   497	            out_dispatch = expert_mlp(  # [C, H]
   498	                x_dispatch,
   499	                moe_w13_local[chunk_index * chunk_experts : (chunk_index + 1) * chunk_experts],
   500	                moe_w2_local[chunk_index * chunk_experts : (chunk_index + 1) * chunk_experts],
   501	                physical_group_sizes,
   502	                active_group_sizes,
   503	                activation_fn,
   504	            )
   505	            # The mirror of dispatch: valid prefixes land back at unclipped sorted positions.
   506	            # Chaining every chunk through one output buffer composes the disjoint writes;
   507	            # dropped rows keep the buffer's zeros, so the final gather-sum reads dropped
   508	            # slots as zero contributions with no expansion step.
   509	            returned = _return_ragged_a2a(
   510	                chunk_capacity,
   511	                out_dispatch,
   512	                returned,
   513	                *return_params,
   514	            )
   515	            accepted_local = accepted_local + jnp.sum(clipped_group_sizes[shard_id], dtype=jnp.int32)
   516	
   517	    with jax.named_scope("combine"):
   518	        out_local = _unpermute_from_global_expert(
   519	            returned,
   520	            sorted_indices,
   521	            combine_weights_local,
   522	            tokens_per_shard=tokens_per_shard,
   523	            topk=topk,
   524	        ).astype(x_local.dtype)
   525	        dropped_local = jnp.sum(group_sizes, dtype=jnp.int32) - accepted_local
   526	        dropped_total = jax.lax.psum(dropped_local, _batch_axes(jax.sharding.get_abstract_mesh()))
   527	    return out_local, CapacityOverflow(sender=dropped_total, receiver=jnp.zeros_like(dropped_total))
    35	    MoeActivation,
    36	    MoeImplementation,
    37	    PspecAxis,
    38	    resolve_moe_implementation,
    39	    split_moe_w13_output,
    40	)
    41	from levanter.grug._moe.ep_common import (
    42	    _clip_receiver_group_sizes as _clip_receiver_group_sizes,
    43	    _expert_granular_a2a_params as _expert_granular_a2a_params,
    44	)
    45	from levanter.grug._moe.ep_deepep import _moe_mlp_ep_deepep_local
    46	from levanter.grug._moe.ep_fixed_all_to_all import _moe_mlp_ep_fixed_a2a_local
    47	from levanter.grug._moe.ep_fixed_pooled_wave_all_to_all import _moe_mlp_ep_fixed_pooled_wave_a2a_local
    48	from levanter.grug._moe.ep_ragged_all_to_all import (
    49	    RAGGED_DEFAULT_EXPERT_CHUNKS as RAGGED_DEFAULT_EXPERT_CHUNKS,
    50	    RAGGED_DISPATCH_REMAT_SAVE_NAMES as RAGGED_DISPATCH_REMAT_SAVE_NAMES,
    51	    _moe_mlp_ep_ragged_a2a_local,
    52	)
    53	from levanter.grug._moe.ep_ring import _moe_mlp_ep_ring_local
    54	from levanter.grug._moe.local import _moe_mlp_local
    55	from levanter.grug.sharding import (
    56	    _batch_spec_from_x,
    57	    _current_mesh,
    58	    _drop_absent_mesh_axes,
    59	    _mesh_axis_size,
    60	    _mesh_has_axis,
    61	    _reshard_for_init,
    62	    _reshard_for_shard_map,
    63	    _value_spec_or_default,
    64	)
    65	from levanter.utils.activation import ActivationFunctionEnum
    66	
    67	
    68	class MoEExpertMlp(eqx.Module):
    69	    """Expert MLP weights for routed MoE calls."""
    70	
    71	    w_gate: jax.Array
    72	    w_up: jax.Array
    73	    w_down: jax.Array
    74	    implementation: MoeImplementation = eqx.field(static=True)
    75	    activation: MoeActivation = eqx.field(static=True)
    76	    capacity_factor: float = eqx.field(static=True)
    77	    pooled_transport_capacity_factor: float | None = eqx.field(static=True, default=None)
    78	    expert_chunks: int = eqx.field(static=True, default=1)
    79	    ragged_expert_chunks: int | None = eqx.field(static=True, default=None)
    80	    num_expert_waves: int = eqx.field(static=True, default=1)
    81	
    82	    @staticmethod
    83	    def init(
    84	        *,
    85	        num_experts: int,
    86	        hidden_dim: int,
    87	        intermediate_dim: int,
    88	        initializer_std: float,
    89	        key: jax.Array,
    90	        gate_up_initializer_std: float | None = None,
    91	        implementation: MoeImplementation | str | None = None,
    92	        activation: MoeActivation = ActivationFunctionEnum.silu,
    93	        capacity_factor: float = _DEFAULT_EP_CAPACITY_FACTOR,
    94	        pooled_transport_capacity_factor: float | None = None,
    95	        expert_chunks: int = 1,
    96	        ragged_expert_chunks: int | None = None,
    97	        num_expert_waves: int = 1,
    98	        pspecs: MoEExpertMlpPspecs = MoEExpertMlpPspecs(),
    99	    ) -> "MoEExpertMlp":
   100	        resolved_implementation = resolve_moe_implementation(implementation)
   101	        k_gate, k_up, k_down = jax.random.split(key, 3)
   102	        # `w_gate`/`w_up` contract over `hidden_dim`, so their fan-in moves when the experts run
   103	        # in a latent space; `w_down` contracts over `intermediate_dim` and is unaffected.
   104	        gate_up_std = initializer_std if gate_up_initializer_std is None else gate_up_initializer_std
   105	        w_gate = _init_weight(k_gate, (num_experts, hidden_dim, intermediate_dim), gate_up_std)
   106	        w_up = _init_weight(k_up, (num_experts, hidden_dim, intermediate_dim), gate_up_std)
   107	        w_down = _reshard_for_init(
   108	            _init_weight(k_down, (num_experts, intermediate_dim, hidden_dim), initializer_std),
   109	            pspecs.w_down,
   110	        )
   111	        return MoEExpertMlp(
   112	            w_gate=_reshard_for_init(w_gate, pspecs.w_gate_up),
   113	            w_up=_reshard_for_init(w_up, pspecs.w_gate_up),
   114	            w_down=w_down,
   115	            implementation=resolved_implementation,
   116	            activation=activation,
   117	            capacity_factor=capacity_factor,
   118	            pooled_transport_capacity_factor=pooled_transport_capacity_factor,
   119	            expert_chunks=expert_chunks,
   120	            ragged_expert_chunks=ragged_expert_chunks,
   121	            num_expert_waves=num_expert_waves,
   122	        )
   123	
   124	    @named_call
   125	    def __call__(
   126	        self,
   127	        x: Float[Array, "T D"],
   128	        selected_experts: Int[Array, "T K"],
   129	        combine_weights: Float[Array, "T K"],
   130	        *,
   131	        mesh: jax.sharding.AbstractMesh | None = None,
   132	        report_capacity_overflow: bool = False,
   133	    ) -> Float[Array, "T D"] | tuple[Float[Array, "T D"], CapacityOverflow]:
   134	        w_gate_up = jnp.concatenate([self.w_gate, self.w_up], axis=-1)
   135	        return moe_mlp(
   136	            x,
   137	            selected_experts,
   138	            combine_weights,
   139	            w_gate_up,
   140	            self.w_down,
   141	            activation=self.activation,
   142	            implementation=self.implementation,
   143	            mesh=mesh,
   144	            capacity_factor=self.capacity_factor,
   145	            pooled_transport_capacity_factor=self.pooled_transport_capacity_factor,
   146	            report_capacity_overflow=report_capacity_overflow,
   147	            expert_chunks=self.expert_chunks,
   148	            ragged_expert_chunks=self.ragged_expert_chunks,
   149	            num_expert_waves=self.num_expert_waves,
   150	        )
   151	
   152	
   153	@named_call
   154	def moe_mlp(
   155	    x: Float[Array, "T D"],
   156	    selected_experts: Int[Array, "T K"],
   157	    combine_weights: Float[Array, "T K"],
   158	    w_up_gate: Float[Array, "E D I2"],
   159	    w_down: Float[Array, "E I D"],
   160	    *,
   161	    activation: MoeActivation = ActivationFunctionEnum.silu,
   162	    implementation: MoeImplementation | str | None = None,
   163	    mesh: jax.sharding.Mesh | jax.sharding.AbstractMesh | None = None,
   164	    capacity_factor: float = _DEFAULT_EP_CAPACITY_FACTOR,
   165	    pooled_transport_capacity_factor: float | None = None,
   166	    report_capacity_overflow: bool = False,
   167	    expert_chunks: int = 1,
   168	    ragged_expert_chunks: int | None = None,
   169	    num_expert_waves: int = 1,
   170	) -> Float[Array, "T D"] | tuple[Float[Array, "T D"], CapacityOverflow]:
   171	    """Functional routed MoE MLP core used by Grug modules and benchmarks.
   172	
   173	    This helper handles dispatch/permute/unpermute (+EP collectives) from
   174	    precomputed token-to-expert assignments. Routing logits/top-k selection
   175	    stays in the caller (e.g. model MLP block).
   176	
   177	    Set `report_capacity_overflow=True` to also return sender and receiver
   178	    counts for expert assignments dropped by EP capacity clipping.
   179	
   180	    `expert_chunks` applies only to the local `sonic_cute` FSDP path. Values
   181	    greater than one split the expert bank into equal, statically sized chunks.
   182	
   183	    `ragged_expert_chunks` is the matching knob for the `ragged_all_to_all` EP
   184	    transport: how many sequential chunks the local expert bank is processed in,
   185	    each with its own share of the receiver capacity. `None` takes
   186	    `RAGGED_DEFAULT_EXPERT_CHUNKS`; an explicit count must divide the local bank.
   187	
   188	    `pooled_transport_capacity_factor` sets the sender capacity for each
   189	    destination pool. `num_expert_waves` sets the static wave count for the
   190	    fixed pooled-wave implementation.
   191	    """
   192	    resolved_implementation = resolve_moe_implementation(implementation)
   193	
   194	    if mesh is None:
   195	        mesh = _current_mesh()
   196	
   197	    if isinstance(activation, ActivationFunctionEnum):
   198	        activation_fn: Callable[[jax.Array], jax.Array] = activation.to_jax_fn()
   199	    else:
   200	        activation_fn = activation
   201	
   202	    if x.ndim != 2:
   203	        raise ValueError(f"x must be rank-2 [T, D], got shape={x.shape}")
   204	    if selected_experts.ndim != 2:
   205	        raise ValueError(f"selected_experts must be rank-2 [T, K], got shape={selected_experts.shape}")
   206	    if selected_experts.shape != combine_weights.shape:
   207	        raise ValueError(
   208	            "selected_experts and combine_weights must have identical [T, K] shapes; "
   209	            f"got {selected_experts.shape} vs {combine_weights.shape}"
   210	        )
   211	    if selected_experts.shape[0] != x.shape[0]:
   212	        raise ValueError(
   213	            f"selected_experts/combine_weights token dim ({selected_experts.shape[0]}) must match x token "
   214	            f"dim ({x.shape[0]})"
   215	        )
   216	
   217	    num_experts = int(w_up_gate.shape[0])
   218	    if w_down.shape[0] != num_experts:
   219	        raise ValueError(
   220	            f"w_down expert dimension ({w_down.shape[0]}) must match w_up_gate expert dimension ({num_experts})"
   221	        )
   222	
   223	    has_expert_axis = _mesh_has_axis(mesh, "expert")
   224	    expert_axis_size = _mesh_axis_size(mesh, "expert")
   225	
   226	    if mesh is None or mesh.empty:
   227	        out, dropped = _moe_mlp_local(
   228	            x,
   229	            selected_experts,
   230	            combine_weights,
   231	            w_up_gate,
   232	            w_down,
   233	            activation_fn=activation_fn,
   234	            num_experts=num_experts,
   235	            implementation=resolved_implementation,
   236	            expert_chunks=expert_chunks,
   237	        )
   238	        if report_capacity_overflow:
   239	            return out, CapacityOverflow(sender=dropped, receiver=jnp.zeros_like(dropped))
   240	        return out
   241	
   242	    batch_spec = _batch_spec_from_x(x, mesh)
   243	
   244	    if has_expert_axis and expert_axis_size > 1:
   245	        if expert_chunks != 1:
   246	            raise ValueError("expert_chunks must be 1 when expert parallelism is active")
   247	        if resolved_implementation not in _EP_MOE_IMPLEMENTATIONS:
   248	            raise ValueError(
   249	                "Local MoE implementations do not yet support expert-parallel collectives; adding EP support "
   250	                "requires a dispatch/combine schedule inside each expert shard plus cross-shard routing. "
   251	                f"got implementation={resolved_implementation!r} with expert axis size={expert_axis_size}"
   252	            )
   253	        if num_experts % expert_axis_size != 0:
   254	            raise ValueError(f"num_experts={num_experts} must be divisible by expert axis size={expert_axis_size}")
   255	
   256	        if resolved_implementation == "ring":
   257	            shard_local_fn = _moe_mlp_ep_ring_local
   258	        elif resolved_implementation == "ragged_all_to_all":
   259	            shard_local_fn = partial(_moe_mlp_ep_ragged_a2a_local, expert_chunks=ragged_expert_chunks)
   260	        elif resolved_implementation == "fixed_all_to_all":
   261	            shard_local_fn = _moe_mlp_ep_fixed_a2a_local
   262	        elif resolved_implementation == "fixed_pooled_wave_all_to_all":
   263	            if pooled_transport_capacity_factor is None:
   264	                raise ValueError("fixed_pooled_wave_all_to_all requires pooled_transport_capacity_factor")
   265	            shard_local_fn = partial(
   266	                _moe_mlp_ep_fixed_pooled_wave_a2a_local,
   267	                transport_capacity_factor=pooled_transport_capacity_factor,
   268	                num_expert_waves=num_expert_waves,
   269	            )
   270	        elif resolved_implementation == "deepep":
   271	            shard_local_fn = _moe_mlp_ep_deepep_local
   272	        else:
   273	            raise AssertionError(f"Unhandled MoE implementation {resolved_implementation!r}")
   274	
   275	        w_up_gate_spec = P("expert", None, None)
   276	        w_down_spec = P("expert", None, None)
   277	
   278	        x = _reshard_for_shard_map(x, mesh, batch_spec)
   279	        selected_experts = _reshard_for_shard_map(selected_experts, mesh, batch_spec)
   280	        combine_weights = _reshard_for_shard_map(combine_weights, mesh, batch_spec)
   281	        w_up_gate = _reshard_for_shard_map(w_up_gate, mesh, w_up_gate_spec)
   282	        w_down = _reshard_for_shard_map(w_down, mesh, w_down_spec)
   283	
   284	        shard_fn = shard_map(
   285	            partial(
   240	    head_dim: int | None = None
   241	    max_seq_len: int = 8192
   242	    sliding_window: int = 2048
   243	    global_every: int = 4
   244	    capacity_factor: float = 1.0
   245	    layer_norm_eps: float = 1e-5
   246	    initializer_std: float = 0.02
   247	    qk_mult: float = 1.3
   248	    sconv: bool = False
   249	    sconv_kernel: int = 4
   250	    sconv_sites: tuple[str, ...] = ("k", "attn", "mlp")
   251	    attention_implementation: GrugAttentionImplementation | None = None
   252	    moe_implementation: MoeImplementation | None = None
   253	    expert_chunks: int = 1
   254	    ragged_expert_chunks: int | None = None
   255	    """Sequential local-expert chunks in the ragged all-to-all transport, each taking its own
   256	    share of the receiver capacity. None takes the backend default
   257	    (`RAGGED_DEFAULT_EXPERT_CHUNKS`, falling back to one chunk on a local bank it does not
   258	    divide); an explicit count must divide the local bank. Other transports ignore it."""
   259	    pooled_transport_capacity_factor: float | None = None
   260	    num_expert_waves: int = 1
   261	    report_capacity_overflow: bool = False
   262	    remat_mode: RematMode = "recompute_all"
   263	    """Per-block gradient checkpointing. "recompute_all" reruns the whole block in
   264	    backward (lowest memory); "save_moe" keeps the tagged MoE dispatch tensors so
   265	    backward skips re-running expert dispatch and its EP collectives; "offload_carry"
   266	    recomputes everything but stages the layer-input residual on pinned host, which
   267	    releases its HBM between forward and backward."""
   268	    remat_save_names: tuple[str, ...] = ()
   269	    """Named intermediates the remat policy keeps on device for the backward, deleting
   270	    their recompute. Names must come from REMAT_SAVEABLE_NAMES; the empty default keeps
   271	    every remat mode's existing policy unchanged. This extends only which named tensors
   272	    are saved -- the offload_carry offload list and the XLA memory-budget flags are
   273	    untouched. Saved values are bit-identical to their recompute; only residency changes."""
   274	    rope: RotaryConfig = dataclasses.field(default_factory=RotaryConfig)
   275	    rope_fused: bool = False
   276	
   277	    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
   278	        _ = self.inferred_head_dim
   279	        if self.num_heads % self.stored_kv_heads != 0:
   280	            raise ValueError("num_heads must be divisible by the stored KV-head count")
   281	        if (self.local_kv_heads is None) != (self.global_kv_heads is None):
   282	            raise ValueError("local_kv_heads and global_kv_heads must be set together")
   283	        if self.local_kv_heads is not None and self.global_kv_heads is not None:
   284	            if self.local_kv_heads <= 0 or self.global_kv_heads <= 0:
   285	                raise ValueError("local_kv_heads and global_kv_heads must be positive")
   286	            if self.num_heads % self.local_kv_heads != 0 or self.num_heads % self.global_kv_heads != 0:
   287	                raise ValueError("num_heads must be divisible by both local and global KV-head counts")
   288	            if self.num_kv_heads != max(self.local_kv_heads, self.global_kv_heads):
   289	                raise ValueError("num_kv_heads must equal the stored maximum of local/global KV heads")
   290	        if self.global_every <= 0:
   291	            raise ValueError("global_every must be positive")
   292	        if self.vocab_size <= 0:
   293	            raise ValueError("vocab_size must be positive")
   294	        if self.max_seq_len <= 0:
   295	            raise ValueError("max_seq_len must be positive")
   296	        if self.num_experts <= 0:
   297	            raise ValueError("num_experts must be positive")
   298	        if self.num_experts_per_token <= 0:
   299	            raise ValueError("num_experts_per_token must be positive")
   300	        if self.num_experts_per_token >= self.num_experts:
   301	            # QB routing takes top-(k+1) and keeps the last entry as the threshold alpha, so a
   302	            # full-bank top-k asks `jax.lax.top_k` for more entries than the router has experts.
   303	            raise ValueError("num_experts_per_token must be < num_experts, because QB routing selects top-(k+1)")
   304	        if self.latent_dim is not None and not 0 < self.latent_dim <= self.hidden_dim:
   305	            raise ValueError(
   306	                f"latent_dim must be in (0, hidden_dim={self.hidden_dim}], got {self.latent_dim}; "
   307	                "it only reduces communication when it is smaller than the hidden dimension"
   308	            )
   309	        if self.shared_expert_intermediate_dim < 0:
   310	            raise ValueError("shared_expert_intermediate_dim must be non-negative")
   311	        if self.capacity_factor <= 0:
   312	            raise ValueError("capacity_factor must be positive")
   313	        if self.expert_chunks <= 0:
   314	            raise ValueError("expert_chunks must be positive")
   315	        if self.ragged_expert_chunks is not None and self.ragged_expert_chunks <= 0:
   316	            raise ValueError("ragged_expert_chunks must be positive")
   317	        if self.pooled_transport_capacity_factor is not None and self.pooled_transport_capacity_factor <= 0:
   318	            raise ValueError("pooled_transport_capacity_factor must be positive")
   319	        if self.moe_implementation == "fixed_pooled_wave_all_to_all":
   320	            if self.pooled_transport_capacity_factor is None:
   321	                raise ValueError("fixed_pooled_wave_all_to_all requires pooled_transport_capacity_factor")
   322	        if self.num_expert_waves <= 0:
   323	            raise ValueError("num_expert_waves must be positive")
   324	        if self.num_shared_experts <= 0:
   325	            raise ValueError("num_shared_experts must be positive")
  1020	        expert_width = cfg.latent_dim if cfg.latent_dim is not None else d
  1021	        latent = cfg.latent_dim
  1022	        return MoEMLP(
  1023	            router=reshard(_init_weight(k_router, (d, e), cfg.initializer_std), P(None, None)),
  1024	            router_bias=jnp.zeros((e,)),
  1025	            w_latent_down=(
  1026	                None
  1027	                if latent is None
  1028	                else reshard(_init_weight(k_down, (d, latent), cfg.initializer_std), P(_FSDP_AXES, "model"))
  1029	            ),
  1030	            latent_norm=None if latent is None else RMSNorm.init(latent, cfg.layer_norm_eps),
  1031	            w_latent_up=(
  1032	                None
  1033	                if latent is None
  1034	                else reshard(_init_weight(k_up, (latent, d), cfg.initializer_std), P("model", _FSDP_AXES))
  1035	            ),
  1036	            expert_mlp=MoEExpertMlp.init(
  1037	                num_experts=cfg.num_experts,
  1038	                hidden_dim=expert_width,
  1039	                intermediate_dim=cfg.intermediate_dim,
  1040	                initializer_std=cfg.initializer_std,
  1041	                key=k_expert,
  1042	                implementation=cfg.moe_implementation,
  1043	                activation=ActivationFunctionEnum.silu,
  1044	                capacity_factor=cfg.capacity_factor,
  1045	                pooled_transport_capacity_factor=cfg.pooled_transport_capacity_factor,
  1046	                expert_chunks=cfg.expert_chunks,
  1047	                ragged_expert_chunks=cfg.ragged_expert_chunks,
  1048	                num_expert_waves=cfg.num_expert_waves,
  1049	            ),
  1050	            cfg=cfg,
  1051	        )
  1052	
  1053	    @named_call
  1054	    def __call__(
  1055	        self,
  1056	        x: Float[Array, "B S D"],
  1057	    ) -> tuple[Float[Array, "B S D"], dict[str, jax.Array]]:
  1058	        b, s, _ = x.shape
  1059	        x_flat = rearrange(x, "b s d -> (b s) d")
  1060	        # Keep the router path in fp32 before top-k, softmax, and QB statistics.
    65	
    66	def build_diagnostic_run(
    67	    *,
    68	    run_id: str,
    69	    restore_from: str | None = None,
    70	    pjrt_wheel: str | None = None,
    71	    dp_racks: int,
    72	    num_steps: int,
    73	    schedule_steps: int | None = None,
    74	    seed: int = 0,
    75	    batch_size: int = HERO_EP_BATCH_SIZE,
    76	    num_experts: int | None = None,
    77	    num_experts_per_token: int | None = None,
    78	    intermediate_dim: int | None = None,
    79	    capacity_factor: float | None = None,
    80	    latent_dim: int | None = None,
    81	    moe_implementation: str | None = None,
    82	    ragged_expert_chunks: int | None = None,
    83	    remat_save: str | None = None,
    84	    master_param_mode: MasterParamMode = HERO_MASTER_PARAM_MODE,
    85	    opt_resident_leaves: int = 0,
    86	    opt_resident_donation: ResidentDonation = ResidentDonation.DONATED,
    87	    processes_per_task: int = HERO_PROCESSES_PER_TASK,
    88	    eval_every: int = 0,
    89	    save_checkpoints: bool = False,
    90	    checkpoint_interval: timedelta = HERO_CHECKPOINT_INTERVAL,
    91	    checkpoint_path: str | None = None,
    92	    checkpoint_debug: CheckpointDebugConfig | None = None,
    93	    watch_interval: int = HERO_WATCH_INTERVAL,
    94	    watch_mode: WatchMode = WatchMode.INLINE,
    95	    profile_steps: int = 0,
    96	    profile_start_step: int = 5,
    97	    training_data_mode: TrainingDataMode = TrainingDataMode.MIXTURE,
    98	    version: str | None = None,
    99	) -> ArtifactStep[HeroThroughputResult]:
   100	    """Build a bounded diagnostic run for the production EP64 hero recipe.
   101	
   102	    The overrides sweep expert count, expert width, routed top-k, and routing capacity from the
   103	    hero spec. They keep the hidden dimension, so the compute-scaled optimizer values stay
   104	    comparable across a sweep. ``None`` keeps the hero value.
   105	
   106	    ``batch_size`` is the global batch across all data-parallel racks. It does not scale with
   107	    ``dp_racks``. ``batch_size`` and ``schedule_steps`` change the token budget for the heuristic.
   108	    """
   109	    if not run_id.strip():
   110	        raise ValueError("run_id must not be empty")
   111	    if dp_racks <= 0:
   112	        raise ValueError(f"dp_racks must be positive, got {dp_racks}")
   113	    if num_steps <= 0:
   114	        raise ValueError(f"num_steps must be positive, got {num_steps}")
   115	    if checkpoint_interval <= timedelta(0):
   116	        raise ValueError(f"checkpoint_interval must be positive, got {checkpoint_interval}")
   117	    if profile_steps < 0:
   118	        raise ValueError(f"profile_steps must be non-negative, got {profile_steps}")
   119	    if profile_start_step < 0:
   120	        raise ValueError(f"profile_start_step must be non-negative, got {profile_start_step}")
   121	    if profile_steps > 0 and profile_start_step >= num_steps:
   122	        raise ValueError(f"profile_start_step must be less than num_steps={num_steps}, got {profile_start_step}")
   123	    # Fail before the rack is allocated: the donation-exclusion diagnostic is defined only for
   124	    # device-resident momentum leaves.
   125	    if opt_resident_donation is ResidentDonation.EXCLUDED and opt_resident_leaves == 0:
   126	        raise ValueError("--opt-resident-donation excluded requires --opt-resident-leaves > 0")
   127	    # `schedule_steps` sets the whole learning-rate schedule; `num_steps` is the absolute step the
   128	    # run stops at (a restore resumes mid-schedule, so it must lie past the restored step).
   129	    # Both matter, and they enter in different places. The optimizer heuristic scales learning rate,
   130	    # adam_lr, and epsilon from a token budget (`num_train_steps * batch * seq`), which fixes the
   131	    # peak. Warmup and decay are *fractions* of `TrainerConfig.num_train_steps`, so that field has to
   132	    # carry the schedule length too -- passing `num_steps` there warms up in `0.01 * num_steps` and
   133	    # decays to `min_lr_ratio` by the end of the short run, which is a whole miniature schedule
   134	    # rather than the head of a long one. Default keeps the two equal, which is the previous behavior.
   135	    if schedule_steps is not None and schedule_steps <= 0:
   136	        raise ValueError(f"schedule_steps must be positive, got {schedule_steps}")
   137	    if schedule_steps is not None and schedule_steps < num_steps:
   138	        raise ValueError(f"schedule_steps={schedule_steps} must be at least num_steps={num_steps}")
   139	    total_schedule_steps = schedule_steps if schedule_steps is not None else num_steps
   140	    _, optimizer = build_hero_configs(
   141	        num_train_steps=total_schedule_steps,
   142	        batch_size=batch_size,
   143	    )
   144	    if remat_save is not None and remat_save not in REMAT_SAVE_BUNDLES:
   145	        raise ValueError(f"remat_save must be one of {sorted(REMAT_SAVE_BUNDLES)}, got {remat_save!r}")
   146	    model = HERO_MODEL_CONFIG
   147	    overrides = {
   148	        name: value
   149	        for name, value in (
   150	            ("num_experts", num_experts),
   151	            ("num_experts_per_token", num_experts_per_token),
   152	            ("intermediate_dim", intermediate_dim),
   153	            ("capacity_factor", capacity_factor),
   154	            ("latent_dim", latent_dim),
   155	            ("moe_implementation", moe_implementation),
   156	            ("ragged_expert_chunks", ragged_expert_chunks),
   157	            ("remat_save_names", REMAT_SAVE_BUNDLES[remat_save] if remat_save is not None else None),
   158	        )
   159	        if value is not None
   160	    }
   161	    if overrides:
   162	        model = dataclasses.replace(model, **overrides)
   163	    model = with_transport_remat_mode(model)
   164	    # A bank that is not divisible by the expert axis fails inside `moe_mlp`, which is after the rack
   165	    # is already allocated and the workspace is built. Reject it here instead.
   166	    if model.num_experts % HERO_EP_EXPERT_AXIS_SIZE != 0:
   167	        raise ValueError(f"num_experts={model.num_experts} must be divisible by {HERO_EP_EXPERT_AXIS_SIZE}")
   168	    local_experts = model.num_experts // HERO_EP_EXPERT_AXIS_SIZE
   169	    if local_experts % model.num_expert_waves != 0:
   170	        raise ValueError(
   171	            f"local expert count={local_experts} must be divisible by num_expert_waves={model.num_expert_waves}"
   172	        )
   173	    # The ragged backend raises on an indivisible chunk count inside the traced step, which is
   174	    # after the rack is allocated. Reject it here instead.
   175	    if model.ragged_expert_chunks is not None and local_experts % model.ragged_expert_chunks != 0:
   176	        raise ValueError(
   177	            f"local expert count={local_experts} must be divisible by expert_chunks={model.ragged_expert_chunks}"
   178	        )
   179	    # The ragged transport requires one GPU per process; fail fast if this is not satisfied.
   180	    if model.moe_implementation == RAGGED_MOE_IMPLEMENTATION and processes_per_task != HERO_GPUS_PER_NODE:
   181	        raise ValueError(
   182	            f"{RAGGED_MOE_IMPLEMENTATION} needs one process per GPU: pass "
   183	            f"processes_per_task={HERO_GPUS_PER_NODE}, got {processes_per_task}"
   184	        )
   185	    pooled = model.moe_implementation == "fixed_pooled_wave_all_to_all"
   186	    if pooled and model.pooled_transport_capacity_factor is None:
   187	        raise AssertionError("the pooled-wave hero requires a transport capacity factor")
   188	    backend_tag = model.moe_implementation.replace("_", "-")
   189	    capacity_tag = f"capacity-{model.capacity_factor:g}"
   190	    # Only the pooled transport has a receiver capacity of its own to report.
   191	    transport_capacity_tags = (f"transport-capacity-{model.pooled_transport_capacity_factor:g}",) if pooled else ()
   192	    wave_tag = f"expert-waves-{model.num_expert_waves}"
   193	    size_tag = f"e{model.num_experts}-i{model.intermediate_dim}"
   194	    remat_save_tags = (f"remat-save-{remat_save.replace('_', '-')}",) if remat_save is not None else ()
   195	    # Only tag a named chunk count; the default arm keeps the tag set it has always had.
   196	    chunk_tags = (f"expert-chunks-{model.ragged_expert_chunks}",) if model.ragged_expert_chunks is not None else ()
   197	    wandb_project = os.environ.get("WANDB_PROJECT") or DEFAULT_WANDB_PROJECT
   198	    grug_trainer = hero_grug_trainer_config(
   199	        replica_axis_size=dp_racks,
   200	        training_data_mode=training_data_mode,
   201	        watch_mode=watch_mode,
   202	        save_checkpoints=save_checkpoints,
   203	        master_param_mode=master_param_mode,
   204	        opt_state_resident_expert_leaves=opt_resident_leaves,
   205	        opt_state_resident_donation=opt_resident_donation,
   206	    )
   207	    train_resources = ResourceConfig.with_gpu(
   208	        "GB200",
   209	        count=HERO_GPUS_PER_NODE,
   210	        cpu=HERO_NODE_CPU,
   390	@click.option(
   391	    "--batch-size",
   392	    type=click.IntRange(min=1),
   393	    default=HERO_EP_BATCH_SIZE,
   394	    show_default=True,
   395	    help="Global sequences per step. This value does not scale with --dp-racks.",
   396	)
   397	@click.option(
   398	    "--moe-implementation",
   399	    default=None,
   400	    help="Override the MoE backend, e.g. ragged_all_to_all. Defaults to the hero spec.",
   401	)
   402	@click.option(
   403	    "--expert-chunks",
   404	    type=click.IntRange(min=1),
   405	    default=None,
   406	    help=(
   407	        "Sequential local-expert chunks in the ragged all-to-all transport, each taking its own "
   408	        "share of the receiver capacity, which also makes capacity clipping per-chunk. Must divide "
   409	        f"the local expert count. Default keeps the backend's {RAGGED_DEFAULT_EXPERT_CHUNKS}. "
   410	        "Other transports ignore it."
   411	    ),
   412	)
   413	@click.option(
   414	    "--remat-save",
   415	    type=click.Choice(sorted(REMAT_SAVE_BUNDLES)),
   416	    default=None,
   417	    help=(
   418	        "Save a curated bundle of named per-token intermediates on device for the backward "
   419	        "instead of recomputing them. Only the remat policy's saved-name list changes; the "
   420	        "layer-carry offload and the XLA memory-budget flags stay untouched. Default keeps "
   421	        "the hero's recompute-everything policy."
   422	    ),
   423	)
   424	@click.option(
   425	    "--master-params",
   426	    type=click.Choice([mode.value for mode in MasterParamMode]),
   427	    default=HERO_MASTER_PARAM_MODE.value,
   428	    show_default=True,
   429	    help=("Where the authoritative fp32 weights live: on device, or as a pinned-host master."),
   430	)
   431	@click.option(
   432	    "--opt-resident-leaves",
   433	    type=click.IntRange(min=0, max=3),
   434	    default=0,
   435	    show_default=True,
   436	    help=(
   437	        "Keep the first N of the offloaded 4D expert momentum shards on device instead of pinned "
   438	        "host (each is 10.87 GiB of HBM at d6144 EP64 and saves its two per-step host copies). "
   439	        "0 offloads all optimizer state, the previous behavior."
   440	    ),
   441	)
   442	@click.option(
   443	    "--opt-resident-donation",
   444	    type=click.Choice([mode.value for mode in ResidentDonation]),
   445	    default=ResidentDonation.DONATED.value,
   446	    show_default=True,
   447	    help=(
   448	        "Diagnostic donation policy for the resident momentum leaves; needs --opt-resident-leaves > 0. "
   449	        "'excluded' keeps them out of the train step's donated state (no input/output aliasing) and "
   450	        "logs per-step debug/resident_ck_in and debug/resident_ck_out canary checksums. Costs one "
   451	        "extra device copy per resident leaf per step (+10.87 GiB transient at d6144 EP64; predicted "
   452	        "peak ~134 GiB at k=1, near the 138.2 GiB threshold -- watch for #8490 allocator churn). "
   453	        "'donated' is the previous, bit-identical behavior."
   454	    ),
   455	)
   456	@click.option(
   457	    "--processes-per-task",
   458	    type=click.IntRange(min=1),
   459	    default=HERO_PROCESSES_PER_TASK,
   460	    show_default=True,
   461	    help="JAX processes per node.",
   462	)
   463	@click.option(
   464	    "--latent-dim",
   465	    type=click.IntRange(min=1),
   550	    help="Fetchable wheel URL (https, presigned object storage) of a self-built jax-cuda13-pjrt; "
   551	    "workers pip-install it after the env sync, replacing the locked wheel. The task log's "
   552	    "'+ jax-cuda13-pjrt==<version>' line is the install custody proof.",
   553	)
   554	@build_options
   555	def main(
   556	    run_id: str,
   557	    restore_from: str | None,
   558	    pjrt_wheel: str | None,
   559	    dp_racks: int,
   560	    num_steps: int,
   561	    schedule_steps: int | None,
   562	    seed: int,
   563	    batch_size: int,
   564	    num_experts: int | None,
   565	    num_experts_per_token: int | None,
   566	    intermediate_dim: int | None,
   567	    capacity_factor: float | None,
   568	    latent_dim: int | None,
   569	    moe_implementation: str | None,
   570	    expert_chunks: int | None,
   571	    remat_save: str | None,
   572	    master_params: str,
   573	    opt_resident_leaves: int,
   574	    opt_resident_donation: str,
   575	    processes_per_task: int,
   576	    save_checkpoints: bool,
   577	    checkpoint_minutes: float,
   578	    checkpoint_path: str | None,
   579	    checkpoint_debug: bool,
   580	    eval_every: int,
   581	    watch_interval: int,
   582	    watch_mode: str,
   583	    profile_steps: int,
   584	    profile_start_step: int,
   585	    training_data: str,
   586	) -> ArtifactStep[HeroThroughputResult]:
   587	    return build_diagnostic_run(
   588	        run_id=run_id,
   589	        restore_from=restore_from,
   590	        pjrt_wheel=pjrt_wheel,
   591	        dp_racks=dp_racks,
   592	        num_steps=num_steps,
   593	        schedule_steps=schedule_steps,
   594	        seed=seed,
   595	        batch_size=batch_size,
   596	        num_experts=num_experts,
   597	        num_experts_per_token=num_experts_per_token,
   598	        intermediate_dim=intermediate_dim,
   599	        capacity_factor=capacity_factor,
   600	        latent_dim=latent_dim,
   601	        moe_implementation=moe_implementation,
   602	        ragged_expert_chunks=expert_chunks,
   603	        remat_save=remat_save,
   604	        master_param_mode=MasterParamMode(master_params),
   605	        opt_resident_leaves=opt_resident_leaves,
   606	        opt_resident_donation=ResidentDonation(opt_resident_donation),
   607	        processes_per_task=processes_per_task,
   608	        save_checkpoints=save_checkpoints,
   609	        checkpoint_interval=timedelta(minutes=checkpoint_minutes),
   610	        checkpoint_path=checkpoint_path,
   611	        checkpoint_debug=(
   612	            CheckpointDebugConfig(
   613	                enabled=True,
   614	                tracemalloc_frames=None,
   615	                top_allocations=0,
   616	                force_gc_before_serialize=False,
   617	                flush_logs=False,
   618	            )
   619	            if checkpoint_debug
   620	            else None

   420	    ep_size = num_experts // local_experts
   421	    tokens_per_shard = x_local.shape[0]
   422	    topk = selected_experts_local.shape[1]
   423	    assignments_per_shard = tokens_per_shard * topk
   424	    local_capacity = int(math.ceil(capacity_factor * assignments_per_shard))
   425	    local_capacity = max(local_experts, local_capacity)
   426	
   427	    # Local experts are processed in sequential chunks so only one chunk's buffers are live at a
   428	    # time: capacity splits evenly across chunks, so two chunks halve the [C, H] transport
   429	    # buffers and the expert MLP's [C, I2] gate-up intermediate that sit between them. It also
   430	    # makes drop clipping per-chunk, which is a routing difference, not just a memory one.
   431	    #
   432	    # What chunking does not change is what crosses the forward/backward boundary. The block's
   433	    # remat policy re-runs this function in the backward, so the lowered module carries every
   434	    # ragged all-to-all three times at any chunk count -- primal, backward recompute, transpose,
   435	    # verified in the lowered StableHLO: 12 ops at two chunks, 6 at one. The a2a outputs are
   436	    # transient inside a chunk, not residuals pinned across the block window.
   437	    chunks = _resolve_expert_chunks(expert_chunks, local_experts)
   438	    chunk_experts = local_experts // chunks
   439	    chunk_capacity = max(chunk_experts, int(math.ceil(local_capacity / chunks)))
   440	    hidden_dim = x_local.shape[1]
   441	
   442	    with jax.named_scope("dispatch"):
   443	        flat_selected = selected_experts_local.reshape(-1)  # [TK]
   444	        sorted_indices = tree_checkpoint_name(jnp.argsort(flat_selected), RAGGED_SORTED_INDICES_REMAT_NAME)  # [TK]
   445	        group_sizes = tree_checkpoint_name(
   446	            jnp.bincount(flat_selected, length=num_experts).astype(jnp.int32), RAGGED_GROUP_SIZES_REMAT_NAME
   447	        )  # [E]
   448	        sorted_x = _gather_dispatch_rows(x_local, sorted_indices, topk)  # [TK, H]
   449	        all_group_sizes = jax.lax.all_gather(group_sizes, "expert")  # [S, E]
   450	
   451	    expert_mlp = _select_expert_mlp(activation_fn)
   452	    chunk_of_expert = (jnp.arange(num_experts, dtype=jnp.int32) % local_experts) // chunk_experts  # [E]
   453	    # Built per-iteration (not jnp.zeros) so the layer scan does not re-copy a hoisted
   454	    # constant into the in-place return a2a's output slot every step; see _loop_local_zeros.
   455	    # The zeros are load-bearing: rows no peer writes back must read 0 in the combine.
   456	    returned = _loop_local_zeros(assignments_per_shard, hidden_dim, x_local.dtype, group_sizes, site=5)  # [TK, H]
   457	    accepted_local = jnp.zeros((), dtype=jnp.int32)
   458	    for chunk_index in range(chunks):
   459	        with jax.named_scope(f"moe_chunk_{chunk_index}"):
   460	            chunk_all_group_sizes = jnp.where(chunk_of_expert[None, :] == chunk_index, all_group_sizes, 0)  # [S, E]
   461	            clipped_group_sizes = _clip_receiver_group_sizes(
   462	                chunk_all_group_sizes,
   463	                local_expert_size=local_experts,
   464	                receiver_capacity=chunk_capacity,
   465	            )
   466	            # Sender starts come from the full (unmasked) sizes, so each chunk reads its
   467	            # groups' accepted prefixes in place in the shared sorted buffer.
   468	            dispatch_params, return_params = _expert_granular_a2a_params(
   469	                all_group_sizes,
   470	                clipped_group_sizes,
   471	                shard_id,
   472	                local_expert_size=local_experts,
   473	            )
   474	            # Serialize the chunks. Without this barrier, the scheduler can start the dispatch
   475	            # of every chunk at the same time. This causes the high memory use that the chunks
   476	            # prevent. A variant that overlaps one transport with the MLP stays within memory.
   477	            # But it does not increase the speed. The transport and the MLP compete for the
   478	            # same SMs.
   479	            chunk_source, _ = jax.lax.optimization_barrier((sorted_x, returned))
   480	            # Accepted rows are the prefix of each unclipped expert group and receiver offsets
   481	            # pack arrivals expert-major, so the received buffer feeds the grouped MLP
   482	            # directly: no sender compaction and no receiver-side permute.
   483	            x_dispatch = _dispatch_ragged_a2a(  # [C, H]
   484	                chunk_capacity,
   485	                assignments_per_shard,
   486	                chunk_source,
   487	                *dispatch_params,
   488	            )
   489	            active_all = jnp.sum(  # [Elocal]
   490	                clipped_group_sizes.reshape(ep_size, ep_size, local_experts)[:, shard_id, :], axis=0
   491	            )
   492	            active_group_sizes = active_all[
   493	                chunk_index * chunk_experts : (chunk_index + 1) * chunk_experts
   494	            ]  # [Echunk]
   495	            total_valid = jnp.sum(active_group_sizes, dtype=jnp.int32)
   496	            physical_group_sizes = active_group_sizes.at[-1].add(chunk_capacity - total_valid)  # [Echunk]
   497	            out_dispatch = expert_mlp(  # [C, H]
   498	                x_dispatch,
   499	                moe_w13_local[chunk_index * chunk_experts : (chunk_index + 1) * chunk_experts],
   500	                moe_w2_local[chunk_index * chunk_experts : (chunk_index + 1) * chunk_experts],
   501	                physical_group_sizes,
   502	                active_group_sizes,
   503	                activation_fn,
   504	            )
   505	            # The mirror of dispatch: valid prefixes land back at unclipped sorted positions.
   506	            # Chaining every chunk through one output buffer composes the disjoint writes;
   507	            # dropped rows keep the buffer's zeros, so the final gather-sum reads dropped
   508	            # slots as zero contributions with no expansion step.
   509	            returned = _return_ragged_a2a(
   510	                chunk_capacity,
   511	                out_dispatch,
   512	                returned,
   513	                *return_params,
   514	            )
   515	            accepted_local = accepted_local + jnp.sum(clipped_group_sizes[shard_id], dtype=jnp.int32)
   516	
   517	    with jax.named_scope("combine"):
   518	        out_local = _unpermute_from_global_expert(
   519	            returned,
   520	            sorted_indices,
   521	            combine_weights_local,
   522	            tokens_per_shard=tokens_per_shard,
   523	            topk=topk,
   524	        ).astype(x_local.dtype)
   525	        dropped_local = jnp.sum(group_sizes, dtype=jnp.int32) - accepted_local
   526	        dropped_total = jax.lax.psum(dropped_local, _batch_axes(jax.sharding.get_abstract_mesh()))
   527	    return out_local, CapacityOverflow(sender=dropped_total, receiver=jnp.zeros_like(dropped_total))
    35	    MoeActivation,
    36	    MoeImplementation,
    37	    PspecAxis,
    38	    resolve_moe_implementation,
    39	    split_moe_w13_output,
    40	)
    41	from levanter.grug._moe.ep_common import (
    42	    _clip_receiver_group_sizes as _clip_receiver_group_sizes,
    43	    _expert_granular_a2a_params as _expert_granular_a2a_params,
    44	)
    45	from levanter.grug._moe.ep_deepep import _moe_mlp_ep_deepep_local
    46	from levanter.grug._moe.ep_fixed_all_to_all import _moe_mlp_ep_fixed_a2a_local
    47	from levanter.grug._moe.ep_fixed_pooled_wave_all_to_all import _moe_mlp_ep_fixed_pooled_wave_a2a_local
    48	from levanter.grug._moe.ep_ragged_all_to_all import (
    49	    RAGGED_DEFAULT_EXPERT_CHUNKS as RAGGED_DEFAULT_EXPERT_CHUNKS,
    50	    RAGGED_DISPATCH_REMAT_SAVE_NAMES as RAGGED_DISPATCH_REMAT_SAVE_NAMES,
    51	    _moe_mlp_ep_ragged_a2a_local,

    53	from levanter.grug._moe.ep_ring import _moe_mlp_ep_ring_local

    55	from levanter.grug.sharding import (
    56	    _batch_spec_from_x,

    58	    _drop_absent_mesh_axes,
    59	    _mesh_axis_size,
